- **absence-audit**: Notice what's deliberately missing from a design. Restraint is a taste signal. Use when studying minimalist products, evaluating design maturity, or understanding the power of removal. ([Dragoon0x/taste-skills](https://github.com/Dragoon0x/taste-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/absence-audit) · ★8 · 2 copies)
- **absence-audit**: Find what's MISSING from a system -- unhandled cases, missing tests, absent monitoring, silent failures. Maps to IN5. ([hummbl-io/agent-skills](https://github.com/hummbl-io/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/absence-audit))
- **absence-case-summary**: Drafts or checks a factual absence case summary for an occupational health referral or a sickness absence review meeting under UK practice, with the absence record, the job's demands, support and reasonable adjustments tried, the sick pay position and the questions for OH, keeping health detail to the minimum the document needs. Use when an HR business partner, HR adviser or manager asks for an OH referral, an occupational health referral letter, a sickness absence case summary, a summary for a stage 1 or stage 2 absence review, a long-term sickness case note, or a check of a referral or abse… ([JordzPope/claude-skills-hr-business-partners](https://github.com/JordzPope/claude-skills-hr-business-partners/tree/HEAD/skills/absence-case-summary))
- **absence-check**: Verify a claim that something does not exist, is dead code, is unused, or has no backup before acting on it. Use whenever you or the user are about to delete, skip, or rebuild something on the grounds that it is absent. ([chocomyong/harness-kit](https://github.com/chocomyong/harness-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/absence-check) · ★2)
- **absence-check**: Verify empty search results, missing files, zero counts, failed jobs, and skipped tests before reporting absence or failure. Use when asked "does this exist", "is anything left", "did it fail", or "did all tests pass" to give a scoped answer supported by live evidence. ([aunysillyme-dev/aunx-skills](https://github.com/aunysillyme-dev/aunx-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/absence-check))
- **absence-claim-verification**: Use when stating or auditing that something does not exist, lacks a property, or is missing, requiring a positive verification of the exact resource before any negative claim. ([otixe/agent-skills](https://github.com/otixe/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/absence-claim-verification))
- **absence-detection**: Use when evaluating any capability claim for silent failure modes — conditions where an implementation fails without raising a test exception or triggering a hook event. Apply before declaring a test suite clean, before finalizing a scholarly review, or whenever you need to know what a test battery cannot see. Invoke this skill for any claim of the form "the implementation handles X" to surface what is structurally invisible to event-driven testing. ([metavacua/AgentRD](https://github.com/metavacua/AgentRD/tree/HEAD/skills/absence-detection))
- **absence-guard-listing-spellings-proves-only-imagination**: 부재 가드를 "내가 본 적 있는 철자 목록"으로 쓰면 상상 밖의 철자로 살아남은 인스턴스를 통과시킨다 — 가드는 green, 결함은 생존. 패턴 목록 대신 **결과 집합(파일/호출자/심볼)을 핀으로 박아라**: 어떤 이름으로든 새 인스턴스가 생기면 목록에 없어서 실패한다. 핀이 썩는 것도 같이 막아라. 트리거 - 부재/금지를 grep 으로 주장하는 테스트, 삭제 PR 의 가드, "이제 아무도 X 를 안 읽는다" 주장, 감사 후속 정리. ([blas1n/claude-skills](https://github.com/blas1n/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/absence-guard-listing-spellings-proves-only-imagination) · ★2)
- **absence-measurement-validity-check**: Before concluding "X doesn't happen" in an integrated system, verify the pipeline that would produce X is actually running. Measuring zero is trivially easy when the producer is off. ([blas1n/claude-skills](https://github.com/blas1n/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/absence-measurement-validity-check) · ★2)
